Dataset › Decisions › 0339-mavan-runs-bitmine-fleet

The validators BitMine has deposited since launching MAVAN, in the fleet deposited at its launch, are run by MAVAN: their weekly deposits match the ETH BitMine says it staked to MAVAN.

AttributionOperator MAVAN · decided 2026-11-18

These 1,785 validators, at 138 of BitMine's withdrawal addresses, are run by MAVAN.

Grounds

  • First partyBitMine says it staked 101,776 ETH to MAVAN, its own staking platform, in the week before its launch on 25 March 2026
  • On chainThese addresses received exactly 101,776 ETH that week, and 191,994 ETH in the week BitMine's staked total rose by 191,994
  • On chainEach address's validators came in one deposit and run one fleet; 99.3% of recent blocks with its Nethermind build are BitMine's
  • First partyMAVAN, BitMine's subsidiary, owns Pier Two, a staking provider (decisions 0254, 0261, 0262)

Explanation

These 1,785 validators are run by MAVAN, the staking business BitMine launched in March 2026. They withdraw to 138 of BitMine's addresses and held 3.36M ETH in November 2026. BitMine remains their allocator, as it chooses MAVAN, its own subsidiary, to run them; this decision records the operator only.

BitMine's account. BitMine announced MAVAN on 25 March 2026 as its "proprietary staking solution", adding: "In this past week, Bitmine staked 101,776 ETH ($219 million) to MAVAN". Its weekly releases give its total staked ETH: 3,142,643 on 24 March and 3,334,637 on 6 April. MAVAN Holdings, 98% owned by BitMine, bought the staking provider Pier Two on 24 March 2026 (decisions 0254, 0261 and 0262).

The match. Five deposit transactions on 21 March 2026 sent exactly 101,776 ETH to these addresses, and in the week of 30 March they received exactly 191,994 ETH, the rise in BitMine's reported total. Each address's validators were deposited in a single transaction, and together they run one fleet: validators signing NM9205, a Nethermind release that other operators had stopped running by then (of the blocks signing it since 12 May 2026, 99.3% are BitMine's), alongside Reth and Geth builds, and in the same transactions validators signing Nimbus's default graffiti. No validator at these addresses signs another operator's name, runs on SSV, or signs the builds of BitMine's other fleets.

What it leaves out. The same addresses received 1.29M ETH of deposits between June 2025 and January 2026, before MAVAN existed; those validators have since exited or been consolidated, and this decision names none of them. Nor does it cover BitMine's validators on SSV (run by Staked's operators), those that move through client builds in step with Lido's MAVAN operator, or those at addresses holding other fleets. The decision would change if BitMine or MAVAN said these validators are run by another firm, or if their builds diverged from the fleet deposited at MAVAN's launch.

What would change it

BitMine or MAVAN saying these validators are run by another firm, or their client builds diverging from the fleet deposited at MAVAN's launch.

Evidence

Transactions and contract calls anyone can repeat on their own node.

  • 0xc81ad99c…c84fa539 — One of five deposit transactions in block 24,701,775 (21 March 2026) that together deposited 101,776 ETH to these addresses: the ETH BitMine staked to MAVAN in its launch week
  • deposits to these addresses in the week of 30 March 2026 — 191,994 ETH: the rise in BitMine's reported staked ETH from 3,142,643 (24 March) to 3,334,637 (6 April)
  • client codes NM9205LH… and Nimbus's default graffiti — Every address holds validators signing NM9205, a Nethermind release others had stopped running: of the blocks signing it since 12 May 2026, when BitMine's validators first did, 99.3% are BitMine's. The others in the same deposit transactions sign Nimbus's default or Geth/Reth codes of the same fleet, and none signs another operator's name or SSV
